- **inductive-reasoning**: Use when patterns, trends, generalizations, or likely rules must be inferred from examples, cases, observations, data points, interviews, events, or repeated signals. Use when applying inductive reasoning, pattern inference, evidence-to-generalization synthesis, or trend interpretation. ([fzfclee/consulting-skills](https://github.com/fzfclee/consulting-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/inductive-reasoning) · ★5)
- **inductive-trace**: Use this skill when the user invokes /inductive-trace or asks for a default public reasoning trace using Inductive Reasoning. The skill writes a durable scratchpad to root memory/{question_name}.md and uses Inductive Reasoning as the actual structure of the analysis. Treat the scale as a rough effort target rather than a fixed quota: around 100 numbered lines or roughly 2,000 to 3,500 tokens of public scratchpad detail. Use this skill when the user wants the answer shaped by evidence generalization instead of a generic response. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/inductive-trace))

- **industriai-voices**: 百业智声 IndustriAI Voices —— AI时代行业访谈提问生成器。融合程前朋友圈39条实战提问逻辑与五大世界级访谈方法论（JTBD切换访谈、The Mom Test、SPIN提问法、动机式访谈MI、调查性报道技巧），为访谈各行各业不同群体（老板、员工、自由职业者、创业者等）生成结构化访谈问题，挖掘AI进入各行业的真实切入点、痛点、焦虑与机遇。当用户需要：(1) 为某个行业/职业/人群设计AI主题访谈提纲，(2) 生成采访问题挖掘AI时代的行业痛点和未来洞见，(3) 设计线下街采/拜访/饭局中的AI话题切入话术，(4) 为"零元挑战""行业探店"等场景准备访谈问题库，(5) 分析某个行业的AI切入机会，(6) 做用户调研/客户访谈/AI需求验证时使用。触发词包括：访谈问题、采访提纲、行业提问、AI访谈、百业智声、IndustriAI、街采问题、探店提问、AI切入点、用户调研、客户访谈。 ([rfdiosuao/industriai-voices](https://github.com/rfdiosuao/industriai-voices/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
